<span style="font-family: Arial;"><span style="color: blue;"><span style="font-size: large;"><b>Q: 首抽這隻好嗎?</b></span></span></span></div>
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">

<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="font-family: Arial;"><b><span style="color: red;"><span style="font-size: large;">A</span></span></b>: 本人而言, [<b>刷(打)不出的卡</b>]開始, 才有他的價值 和 名正言順的[<b>稀有</b>]</span></div>

text-decoration: none;" target="_blank"><span style="color: #3e81c6;"></span></a><span style="color: #3e81c6;"><a class="T3_red" href="http://images4.wikia.nocookie.net/__cb20130131075244/tos/zh/images/thumb/c/cc/189i.png/60px-189i.png" id="http://images4.wikia.nocookie.net/__cb20130131075244/tos/zh/images/thumb/c/cc/189i.png/60px-189i.png" name="attachImgName" style="color: #3e81c6; text-decoration: none;" type="height=60&amp;width=60"><img border="0" height="60" src="http://images4.wikia.nocookie.net/__cb20130131075244/tos/zh/images/thumb/c/cc/189i.png/60px-189i.png" style="border: 0px; margin: 5px 0px; max-width: 645px;" width="60" /></a></span><a href="http://ref.gamer.com.tw/redir.php?url=http%3A%2F%2Fimages1.wikia.nocookie.net%2F__cb20130221082561%2Ftos%2Fzh%2Fimages%2Fthumb%2Fd%2Fdc%2F190i.png%2F60px-190i.png" style="color: #3e81c6; text-decoration: none;" target="_blank"><span style="color: #3e81c6;"></span></a><span style="color: #3e81c6;"><a class="T3_red" href="http://images1.wikia.nocookie.net/__cb20130221082561/tos/zh/images/thumb/d/dc/190i.png/60px-190i.png" id="http://images1.wikia.nocookie.net/__cb20130221082561/tos/zh/images/thumb/d/dc/190i.png/60px-190i.png" name="attachImgName" style="color: #3e81c6; text-decoration: none;" type="height=60&amp;width=60"><img border="0" height="60" src="http://images1.wikia.nocookie.net/__cb20130221082561/tos/zh/images/thumb/d/dc/190i.png/60px-190i.png" style="border: 0px; margin: 5px 0px; max-width: 645px;" width="60" /></a></span></span></div>
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="font-family: Arial;">此類暫時只可以抽卡獲得, 雖然技能不算OP, 但實用的[<b>X屬2倍攻擊</b>]最小可順利到4封</span></div>
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="font-family: Arial;">但不建議首抽開始, 因為之後抽石卡(魔法封印石)很高機率會再出</span></div>
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="font-family: Arial;"><br /></span></div>
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="font-family: Arial;">至於還有<b>巨象系列</b>&nbsp;(No.146-160)<a href="http://ref.gamer.com.tw/redir.php?url=http%3A%2F%2Fimages3.wikia.nocookie.net%2F__cb20130131074856%2Ftos%2Fzh%2Fimages%2Fthumb%2F6%2F66%2F148i.png%2F60px-148i.png" style="color: #3e81c6; text-decoration: none;" target="_blank"><span style="color: #3e81c6;"></span></a><span style="color: #3e81c6;"><a class="T3_red" href="http://images3.wikia.nocookie.net/__cb20130131074856/tos/zh/images/thumb/6/66/148i.png/60px-148i.png" id="http://images3.wikia.nocookie.net/__cb20130131074856/tos/zh/images/thumb/6/66/148i.png/60px-148i.png" name="attachImgName" style="color: #3e81c6; text-decoration: none;" type="height=60&amp;width=60"><img border="0" height="60" src="http://images3.wikia.nocookie.net/__cb20130131074856/tos/zh/images/thumb/6/66/148i.png/60px-148i.png" style="border: 0px; margin: 5px 0px; max-width: 645px;" width="60" /></a></span><a href="http://ref.gamer.com.tw/redir.php?url=http%3A%2F%2Fimages2.wikia.nocookie.net%2F__cb20130131074857%2Ftos%2Fzh%2Fimages%2Fthumb%2F3%2F39%2F151i.png%2F60px-151i.png" style="color: #3e81c6; text-decoration: none;" target="_blank"><span style="color: #3e81c6;"></span></a><span style="color: #3e81c6;"><a class="T3_red" href="http://images2.wikia.nocookie.net/__cb20130131074857/tos/zh/images/thumb/3/39/151i.png/60px-151i.png" id="http://images2.wikia.nocookie.net/__cb20130131074857/tos/zh/images/thumb/3/39/151i.png/60px-151i.png" name="attachImgName" style="color: #3e81c6; text-decoration: none;" target="_blank"><span style="color: #3e81c6;"></span></a><span style="color: #3e81c6;"><a class="T3_red" href="http://images4.wikia.nocookie.net/__cb20130131074858/tos/zh/images/thumb/f/f5/157i.png/60px-157i.png" id="http://images4.wikia.nocookie.net/__cb20130131074858/tos/zh/images/thumb/f/f5/157i.png/60px-157i.png" name="attachImgName" style="color: #3e81c6; margin: 5px 0px; max-width: 645px;" width="60" /></a></span></span></div>
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="font-family: Arial;">及<b>機械獸系列&nbsp;</b>(No.161-175)<a href="http://ref.gamer.com.tw/redir.php?url=http%3A%2F%2Fimages2.wikia.nocookie.net%2F__cb20130131075012%2Ftos%2Fzh%2Fimages%2Fthumb%2Fc%2Fc6%2F163i.png%2F60px-163i.png" style="color: #3e81c6; text-decoration: none;" target="_blank"><span style="color: #3e81c6;"></span></a><span style="color: #3e81c6;"><a class="T3_red" href="http://images2.wikia.nocookie.net/__cb20130131075012/tos/zh/images/thumb/c/c6/163i.png/60px-163i.png" id="http://images2.wikia.nocookie.net/__cb20130131075012/tos/zh/images/thumb/c/c6/163i.png/60px-163i.png" name="attachImgName" style="color: #3e81c6; text-decoration: none;" type="height=60&amp;width=60"><img border="0" height="60" src="http://images2.wikia.nocookie.net/__cb20130131075012/tos/zh/images/thumb/c/c6/163i.png/60px-163i.png" style="border: 0px; margin: 5px 0px; max-width: 645px;" width="60" /></a></span><a href="http://ref.gamer.com.tw/redir.php?url=http%3A%2F%2Fimages3.wikia.nocookie.net%2F__cb20130131075013%2Ftos%2Fzh%2Fimages%2Fthumb%2Fb%2Fb8%2F166i.png%2F60px-166i.png" style="color: #3e81c6; text-decoration: none;" target="_blank"><span style="color: #3e81c6;"></span></a><span style="color: #3e81c6;"><a class="T3_red" href="http://images3.wikia.nocookie.net/__cb20130131075013/tos/zh/images/thumb/b/b8/166i.png/60px-166i.png" id="http://images3.wikia.nocookie.net/__cb20130131075013/tos/zh/images/thumb/b/b8/166i.png/60px-166i.png" name="attachImgName" style="color: #3e81c6; text-decoration: none;" type="height=60&amp;width=60"><img border="0" height="60" src="http://images3.wikia.nocookie.net/__cb20130131075013/tos/zh/images/thumb/b/b8/166i.png/60px-166i.png" style="border: 0px; margin: 5px 0px; max-width: 645px;" width="60" /></a></span><a href="http://ref.gamer.com.tw/redir.php?url=http%3A%2F%2Fimages1.wikia.nocookie.net%2F__cb20130131075014%2Ftos%2Fzh%2Fimages%2Fthumb%2F1%2F11%2F169i.png%2F60px-169i.png" style="color: #3e81c6; text-decoration: none;" target="_blank"><span style="color: #3e81c6;"></span></a><span style="color: #3e81c6;"><a class="T3_red" href="http://images1.wikia.nocookie.net/__cb20130131075014/tos/zh/images/thumb/1/11/169i.png/60px-169i.png" id="http://images1.wikia.nocookie.net/__cb20130131075014/tos/zh/images/thumb/1/11/169i.png/60px-169i.png" name="attachImgName" style="color: #3e81c6; text-decoration: none;" type="height=60&amp;width=60"><img border="0" height="60" src="http://images1.wikia.nocookie.net/__cb20130131075014/tos/zh/images/thumb/1/11/169i.png/60px-169i.png" style="border: 0px; margin: 5px 0px; max-width: 645px;" width="60" /></a></span><a href="http://ref.gamer.com.tw/redir.php?url=http%3A%2F%2Fimages1.wikia.nocookie.net%2F__cb20130131075015%2Ftos%2Fzh%2Fimages%2Fthumb%2F1%2F1c%2F172i.png%2F60px-172i.png" style="color: #3e81c6; text-decoration: none;" target="_blank"><span style="color: #3e81c6;"></span></a><span style="color: #3e81c6;"><a class="T3_red" href="http://images1.wikia.nocookie.net/__cb20130131075015/tos/zh/images/thumb/1/1c/172i.png/60px-172i.png" id="http://images1.wikia.nocookie.net/__cb20130131075015/tos/zh/images/thumb/1/1c/172i.png/60px-172i.png" name="attachImgName" style="color: #3e81c6; text-decoration: none;" type="height=60&amp;width=60"><img border="0" height="60" src="http://images1.wikia.nocookie.net/__cb20130131075015/tos/zh/images/thumb/1/1c/172i.png/60px-172i.png" style="border: 0px; margin: 5px 0px; max-width: 645px;" width="60" /></a></span><a href="http://ref.gamer.com.tw/redir.php?url=http%3A%2F%2Fimages3.wikia.nocookie.net%2F__cb20130131075016%2Ftos%2Fzh%2Fimages%2Fthumb%2Fa%2Fae%2F175i.png%2F60px-175i.png" style="color: #3e81c6; text-decoration: none;" target="_blank"><span style="color: #3e81c6;"></span></a><span style="color: #3e81c6;"><a class="T3_red" href="http://images3.wikia.nocookie.net/__cb20130131075016/tos/zh/images/thumb/a/ae/175i.png/60px-175i.png" id="http://images3.wikia.nocookie.net/__cb20130131075016/tos/zh/images/thumb/a/ae/175i.png/60px-175i.png" name="attachImgName" style="color: #3e81c6; text-decoration: none;" type="height=60&amp;width=60"><img border="0" height="60" src="http://images3.wikia.nocookie.net/__cb20130131075016/tos/zh/images/thumb/a/ae/175i.png/60px-175i.png" style="border: 0px; margin: 5px 0px; max-width: 645px;" width="60" /></a></span>(只列最後進化樣子)</span></div>
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="font-family: Arial;">這兩系列<b>不可以於正常關卡刷出,&nbsp;</b>但可能於每周活動關卡出現並可取得</span></div>
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="font-family: Arial;">(換言之, 不課金不抽卡的話, 要集一套可能要數周至數個月)</span></div>
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="font-family: Arial;">以上兩系也不建議首抽開始 (覺得巨象的轉珠技是十分有用的不在此限)</span></div>
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="font-family: Arial;"><br /></span></div>
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="font-family: Arial;">其他沒列出的, 不是[<b>因為可以刷得出</b>], 就是[<b>實力不太好</b>]而沒列出.</span></div>
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="font-family: Arial;">如果是想<b>高效率破關而無視珍貴與否</b>, 請自行找資料看看<b>那隻角色是適合自己風格</b>的[<b>強</b>].</span></div>
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="font-family: Arial;"><br /></span></div>
<div style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px; text-align: -webkit-center;">
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="font-family: Arial;"><span style="color: blue;"><span style="font-size: large;"><b>Q: 主動技(Active Skill) 和 隊長技 (Leader Skill)有什麼分別?</b></span></span></span></div>
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="font-family: Arial;"><br /></span></div>
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="font-family: Arial;"><b><span style="color: red;"><span style="font-size: large;">A</span></span></b>:&nbsp;<b>主動技:</b>&nbsp;放於隊伍內, 在一定回合之後(按技能及等級而定),</span></div>
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="font-family: Arial;">看到角色圖示在閃動就是可以使用主動技了, 每次戰鬥不限使用次數, 回合到就可以使用</span></div>
</div>
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="font-family: Arial;"><b>隊長技</b>(或叫被動技): 必須放於隊伍首位(最左邊的第一位)才可以發動,</span></div>
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="font-family: Arial;">此技能的效果由戰鬥開始到結束時也會持續生效.</span></div>
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="font-family: Arial;"><br /></span></div>
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="font-family: Arial;">而帶出戰鬥的戰友(朋友),也會有隊長技效果, 效果可重疊,</span></div>
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
例如己方是[2.5倍攻擊力]提升, 朋友也是[2.5倍攻擊力]提升</div>
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
那這隊的效果就是 6.25倍(2.5 x 2.5)攻擊力提升了</div>
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
這最終的效果不會顯示於任何畫面內, 只可分別確認你跟朋友的隊長技</div>
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
(註: 這戰友必須是[朋友], 而不是[冒險者], 未成為朋友的戰友不會有隊長技效果.)</div>
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<br /></div>
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
詳細可仔細了解新手教學</div>
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="font-family: Arial;"><br /></span></div>
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="font-family: Arial;"><b><span style="font-size: large;"><span style="color: blue;">Q: 這樣的牌, 怎組隊伍?</span></span></b></span></div>
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="font-family: Arial;"><br /></span></div>
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="font-family: Arial;"><span style="color: red;"><span style="font-size: large;"><b>A:</b></span></span>&nbsp;首先, 必須了解自己背包上的卡, 那個角色的<b>隊長技能</b>可以有效的發揮你的隊伍</span></div>
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="font-family: Arial;">最最最低限度的隊伍, 就是你開始遊戲時選擇的<b>水木火主之一組成的 [純色隊]</b></span></div>
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="font-family: Arial;">例如你用<span style="color: red;">莫莉(水主)</span>, 那最小可以組<span style="color: red;">1.5倍攻的水隊</span>了, 朋友也多加<span style="color: red;">水兩倍角色</span>.</span></div>
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="font-family: Arial;">去水屬關刷一下水屬角色, 最小也有3倍攻的水隊了(你的1.5+隊友2倍)</span></div>
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="font-family: Arial;"><br /></span></div>
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="font-family: Arial;">真的想換個玩法嗎? 還是先看自己有什麼角色, 看一下大之的角色(如攻高, 血多, 稀有度高)</span></div>
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="font-family: Arial;">看看他們的隊長技能, 去配合打法, 例如:</span></div>
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="font-family: Arial;"><a href="http://ref.gamer.com.tw/redir.php?url=http%3A%2F%2Ftruth.bahamut.com.tw%2Fs01%2F201304%2F217f176a30c4a6e27a9c35b973b1e79e.JPG" style="color: #3e81c6; text-decoration: none;" target="_blank"><span style="color: #3e81c6;"></span></a><span style="color: #3e81c6;"><a class="T3_red" href="http://truth.bahamut.com.tw/s01/201304/217f176a30c4a6e27a9c35b973b1e79e.JPG" id="http://truth.bahamut.com.tw/s01/201304/217f176a30c4a6e27a9c35b973b1e79e.JPG" name="attachImgName" style="color: #3e81c6; text-decoration: none;" type=""><img border="0" src="http://truth.bahamut.com.tw/s01/201304/217f176a30c4a6e27a9c35b973b1e79e.JPG" style="border: 0px; margin: 5px 0px; max-width: 645px;" /></a></span></span></div>
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="font-family: Arial;">這張角色的隊長技就是 [野獸之意志] 效果是 [<span style="color: red;">獸類生命力2.5倍</span>]</span></div>
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="font-family: Arial;">這意味著, 如果用這張當隊長(放最左邊第一格), 所有的獸類隊員也會有2.5倍的生命力</span></div>
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="font-family: Arial;">也即是說, 你的牌組內再<span style="color: red;">多一點獸類的角色, 就可以組一隊多血的隊伍</span>了</span></div>
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="font-family: Arial;"><br /></span></div>
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="font-family: Arial;">再舉一個例子:</span></div>
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="font-family: Arial;"><a href="http://ref.gamer.com.tw/redir.php?url=http%3A%2F%2Ftruth.bahamut.com.tw%2Fs01%2F201304%2Fdf599a69c6d16c4b2492e14e40ebe1c8.JPG" style="color: #3e81c6; text-decoration: none;" target="_blank"><span style="color: #3e81c6;"></span></a><span style="color: #3e81c6;"><a class="T3_red" href="http://truth.bahamut.com.tw/s01/201304/df599a69c6d16c4b2492e14e40ebe1c8.JPG" id="http://truth.bahamut.com.tw/s01/201304/df599a69c6d16c4b2492e14e40ebe1c8.JPG" name="attachImgName" style="color: #3e81c6; text-decoration: none;" type=""><img border="0" src="http://truth.bahamut.com.tw/s01/201304/df599a69c6d16c4b2492e14e40ebe1c8.JPG" style="border: 0px; margin: 5px 0px; max-width: 645px;" /></a></span></span></div>
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="font-family: Arial;">隊長技是 [狐仙軍神] 效果是[全體攻擊力名回復力1.5倍]</span></div>
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="font-family: Arial;">這簡單的意味著, 只要他是隊長, 就算帶什麼角色出去也有1.5倍的傷害了,</span></div>
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="font-family: Arial;">那就帶多點高攻的隊友吧</span></div>
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="font-family: Arial;"><br /></span></div>
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="font-family: Arial;"><b><span style="font-size: large;"><span style="color: blue;">Q: 看了例子, 還不知道怎樣組嗎?</span></span></b></span></div>
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="font-family: Arial;"><b><span style="font-size: large;"><span style="color: blue;"><br /></span></span></b></span></div>
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="font-family: Arial;"><b><span style="color: red;"><span style="font-size: large;">A:</span></span></b>那就要看你想怎組了, 你知道x隊很強~自己也想這樣玩嗎?</span></div>
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="font-family: Arial;">那你就要先了解怎樣把這角色弄到手了</span></div>
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="font-family: Arial;">詳細每張的請看其他文章說明每張卡的出處 (<a href="http://ref.gamer.com.tw/redir.php?url=http%3A%2F%2Fforum.gamer.com.tw%2FC.php%3Fbsn%3D23805%26snA%3D5832%26tnum%3D11" style="color: #3e81c6; text-decoration: none;" target="_blank"><span style="color: #3e81c6;">我跳</span></a>)</span></div>
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="font-family: Arial;"><br /></span></div>
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="font-family: Arial;"><span style="color: blue;"><span style="font-size: large;"><b>Q: 果真沒有好卡的話怎麼辦?</b></span></span></span></div>
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="font-family: Arial;"><span style="color: blue;"><span style="font-size: large;"><b><br /></b></span></span></span></div>
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="font-family: Arial;"><b><span style="color: red;"><span style="font-size: large;">A:</span></span></b></span></div>
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="font-family: Arial;">1: 課金抽石卡抽到為止</span></div>
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="font-family: Arial;">2: 花時間刷到出卡為止</span></div>
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="font-family: Arial;">這其實已解答了8成新手玩家的基本問題</span></div>
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="font-family: Arial;"><br /></span></div>
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="font-family: Arial;">如果真的決定了課金(用現金買魔法石封卡), 那建議<span style="color: red;"><b>早期抽</b></span></span></div>
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="font-family: Arial;">免得他日你花了幾星期刷到角色出來了,</span></div>
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="font-family: Arial;">但之後手一滑就把他抽出來重覆了(石抽普遍是可以抽到大部份刷得出的戰鬥角色)</span></div>
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="font-family: Arial;"><br /></span></div>
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="font-family: Arial;"><b><span style="color: red;">不努力, 也不花錢 那要強勁隊伍是急不來的</span></b></span></div>
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="font-family: Arial;"><b><span style="color: red;"><br /></span></b></span></div>
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="font-family: Arial;"><b><span style="color: blue;"><span style="font-size: large;">Q:&nbsp;</span></span></b><b><span style="color: blue;"><span style="font-size: large;">課金了, 不是沒有[好卡] ,就是不知道怎配合</span></span></b></span></div>
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="font-family: Arial;"><b><span style="color: red;"><span style="font-size: large;">A:&nbsp;</span></span></b><span style="font-size: small;">例如,&nbsp;</span>那天你首抽加之後4抽是&nbsp;<b>水埃+海龍+阿提蜜斯(暗希)+悟空+木妖精</b></span></div>
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="font-family: Arial;">4個神加一個妖精的<span style="color: red;">四不搭情況</span>,</span></div>
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="font-family: Arial;">海龍沒進化沒有對神有加成</span></div>
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="font-family: Arial;">水埃只是打火屬有點用處</span></div>
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="font-family: Arial;">暗希只是高攻和難卡暗珠</span></div>
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="font-family: Arial;">悟空只是減傷</span></div>
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="font-family: Arial;">木妖只是+妖精生命力</span></div>
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="font-family: Arial;">那怎辦?</span></div>
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="font-family: Arial;"><b>沒人叫你一次要把最稀有的同時放進隊伍 (更別說沒有隊伍空間)</b></span></div>
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="font-family: Arial;">分開來看:</span></div>
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="font-family: Arial;">海龍進化前還有對龍有2倍攻加乘, 那就先找龍類角色進隊</span></div>
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="font-family: Arial;">其他看情況 如打火屬的關卡時也可以轉用水埃+水屬隊員</span></div>
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="font-family: Arial;"><br /></span></div>
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="font-family: Arial;"><b><span style="color: red;">先看眼前情況想要怎樣的效果, 就可以組出自己的隊伍了</span></b></span></div>
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="font-family: Arial;"><b><span style="color: red;"><br /></span></b></span></div>
<div style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px; text-align: -webkit-center;">
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<b><span style="font-size: large;"><span style="color: blue;">Q: 如何有效率地利用魔法石?</span></span></b></div>
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<br /></div>
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="color: red;"><span style="font-size: large;"><b>A:&nbsp;</b></span></span>一般玩家, 本人建議:&nbsp;<b>擴背包 &gt; 增好友上限 &gt;= 回體力 &gt; 抽卡 &gt; 復活</b></div>
</div>
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<br /></div>
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
擴大了背包, 你才可以放心地連刷, 不用打一場就要賣卡片或[勉強]為了空間強化卡片</div>
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<br /></div>
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
擴好友上限, 一來可以另你選擇出戰的隊友多了, 二來也可更高收益的賺取友情點</div>
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<br /></div>
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
回體力, 一般也已算很小用一般是拿來刷限時的關卡(如緊急任務)</div>
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
或真的太無聊沒事做補個體力再刷, 但建議高等級才用效率好點</div>
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
(前期一粒回20體,後期是一粒回數十體喔)</div>
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<br /></div>
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
為什麼抽卡不重要? 想抽的話, 最好<span style="color: red;">前期抽</span>, 盡快令隊伍成形</div>
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
不然到後期你已刷了很多卡出來, 卻又<span style="color: red;">很高機會抽到重覆</span>了</div>
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
要抽 盡快抽, 或有活動再抽</div>
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
(不負責任調查, 抽石卡得到[<span style="color: red;">刷不出的卡</span>](神類{不算妹子},2.0妖精,2.0異界龍,沒算西方魔獸)</div>
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
機率大約<span style="color: red;"><b>10%~15%左右</b></span>, 算西方魔獸的話<b>20-25%吧</b>)(別問我為什麼)</div>
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<br /></div>
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
復活: 基本只有一種情況下用, 就是一些超超超超難打的關卡,</div>
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
但破了可能會有的獨有角色(老玩家會知)</div>
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
否則一般情況下, 復活一次的石如果拿來補充體力, 也夠你挑戰很多次了</div>
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<br /></div>
<div style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px; text-align: -webkit-center;">
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="font-family: Arial;"><b><span style="color: blue;"><span style="font-size: large;">Q:&nbsp;</span></span></b><b><span style="color: blue;"><span style="font-size: large;">如何有效率地運用背包, 多少格才好?</span></span></b></span></div>
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="font-family: Arial;"><b><span style="color: blue;"><span style="font-size: large;"><br /></span></span></b></span></div>
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="font-family: Arial;"><span style="font-size: large;"><span style="color: red;"><b>A:</b></span></span><span style="font-size: small;">&nbsp;如果有無限的魔法石, 怎樣也會建議你先把背包擴至最大</span></span></div>
</div>
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="font-family: Arial;">同上題說, 你不用為了空間而勉強強化或賣卡片</span></div>
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="font-family: Arial;">背包滿時, 又勉強將幾隻3000不同屬的肥料吃掉~是很浪費的</span></div>
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="font-family: Arial;"><br /></span></div>
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="font-family: Arial;">其次是, 打到比較難打的進化材料, 也可以不用因為[用不著]而先賣/吃掉</span></div>
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
賣了要用的進化材料, 那就要不知浪費多少體力刷回了</div>
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="font-family: Arial;"><br /></span></div>
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="font-family: Arial;">建議: 除了&nbsp;<span style="color: red;">出戰(收集)卡片(N), 留著未來用的[進化材料] 約30格, 再加10-25格的空間</span>做緩衝就好了</span></div>
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="font-family: Arial;">就筆者本人而言, 出戰角色約60 , 進化材料 20(沒什麼好進化了),</span></div>
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="font-family: Arial;">20格放強化材料及緩衝剛好100格, 當然也是有點勉強的</span></div>
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="font-family: Arial;"><br /></span></div>
<div style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px; text-align: -webkit-center;">
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="font-family: Arial;"><b><span style="color: blue;"><span style="font-size: large;">Q: [</span></span></b><b><span style="color: blue;"><span style="font-size: large;">強化]應該怎做才好?</span></span></b></span></div>
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="font-family: Arial;"><b><span style="color: blue;"><span style="font-size: large;"><br /></span></span></b></span></div>
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="font-family: Arial;"><span style="font-size: large;"><span style="color: red;"><b>A:</b></span></span><span style="font-size: small;">&nbsp;板上有很多文章討論[最有效]的堆肥方法, 在這不極端的去每個細節算給你如何最有效</span></span></div>
</div>
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="font-family: Arial;">記著幾點就好:</span></div>
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="font-family: Arial;"><b>1:&nbsp;</b>主要想強化的角色(A) 不要直接吃普通肥料(B)強化, 應該先把肥料(B)吃更低級的肥料(C)肥一點再吃</span></div>
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="font-family: Arial;"><b>2:</b>&nbsp;最佳的肥料(B)是 小魔女系列</span><a class="T3_red" href="http://images1.wikia.nocookie.net/__cb20130131074161/tos/zh/images/thumb/6/6d/087i.png/60px-087i.png" id="http://images1.wikia.nocookie.net/__cb20130131074161/tos/zh/images/thumb/6/6d/087i.png/60px-087i.png" name="attachImgName" style="color: #3e81c6; text-decoration: none;" type="height=60&amp;width=60"><img border="0" height="60" src="http://images1.wikia.nocookie.net/__cb20130131074161/tos/zh/images/thumb/6/6d/087i.png/60px-087i.png" style="border: 0px; margin: 5px 0px; max-width: 645px;" width="60" /></a><a class="T3_red" href="http://images1.wikia.nocookie.net/__cb20130131074202/tos/zh/images/thumb/c/c5/089i.png/60px-089i.png" id="http://images1.wikia.nocookie.net/__cb20130131074202/tos/zh/images/thumb/c/c5/089i.png/60px-089i.png" name="attachImgName" style="color: #3e81c6; text-decoration: none;" type="height=60&amp;width=60"><img border="0" height="60" src="http://images1.wikia.nocookie.net/__cb20130131074202/tos/zh/images/thumb/c/c5/089i.png/60px-089i.png" style="border: 0px; text-decoration: none;" type="height=60&amp;width=60"><img border="0" height="60" src="http://images2.wikia.nocookie.net/__cb20130131074203/tos/zh/images/thumb/e/ec/093i.png/60px-093i.png" style="border: 0px; margin: 5px 0px; max-width: 645px;" width="60" /></a><a class="T3_red" href="http://images4.wikia.nocookie.net/__cb20130131074203/tos/zh/images/thumb/2/24/095i.png/60px-095i.png" id="http://images4.wikia.nocookie.net/__cb20130131074203/tos/zh/images/thumb/2/24/095i.png/60px-095i.png" name="attachImgName" style="color: #3e81c6; text-decoration: none;" type="height=60&amp;width=60"><img border="0" height="60" src="http://images4.wikia.nocookie.net/__cb20130131074203/tos/zh/images/thumb/2/24/095i.png/60px-095i.png" style="border: 0px; margin: 5px 0px; max-width: 645px;" width="60" /></a></div>
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
其次為 精靈系列<a class="T3_red" href="http://images1.wikia.nocookie.net/__cb20130131074005/tos/zh/images/thumb/0/04/067i.png/60px-067i.png" id="http://images1.wikia.nocookie.net/__cb20130131074005/tos/zh/images/thumb/0/04/067i.png/60px-067i.png" name="attachImgName" style="color: #3e81c6; text-decoration: none;" type="height=60&amp;width=60"><img border="0" height="60" src="http://images1.wikia.nocookie.net/__cb20130131074005/tos/zh/images/thumb/0/04/067i.png/60px-067i.png" style="border: 0px; margin: 5px 0px; max-width: 645px;" width="60" /></a><a class="T3_red" href="http://images4.wikia.nocookie.net/__cb20130131074005/tos/zh/images/thumb/3/33/069i.png/60px-069i.png" id="http://images4.wikia.nocookie.net/__cb20130131074005/tos/zh/images/thumb/3/33/069i.png/60px-069i.png" name="attachImgName" style="color: #3e81c6; text-decoration: none;" type="height=60&amp;width=60"><img border="0" height="60" src="http://images4.wikia.nocookie.net/__cb20130131074005/tos/zh/images/thumb/3/33/069i.png/60px-069i.png" style="border: 0px; text-decoration: none;" type="height=60&amp;width=60"><img border="0" height="60" src="http://images2.wikia.nocookie.net/__cb20130131074006/tos/zh/images/thumb/c/c1/071i.png/60px-071i.png" style="border: 0px; margin: 5px 0px; max-width: 645px;" width="60" /></a><a class="T3_red" href="http://images3.wikia.nocookie.net/__cb20130131074006/tos/zh/images/thumb/6/64/073i.png/60px-073i.png" id="http://images3.wikia.nocookie.net/__cb20130131074006/tos/zh/images/thumb/6/64/073i.png/60px-073i.png" name="attachImgName" style="color: #3e81c6; text-decoration: none;" type="height=60&amp;width=60"><img border="0" height="60" src="http://images3.wikia.nocookie.net/__cb20130131074006/tos/zh/images/thumb/6/64/073i.png/60px-073i.png" style="border: 0px; margin: 5px 0px; max-width: 645px;" width="60" /></a><a class="T3_red" href="http://images2.wikia.nocookie.net/__cb20130131074007/tos/zh/images/thumb/4/48/075i.png/60px-075i.png" id="http://images2.wikia.nocookie.net/__cb20130131074007/tos/zh/images/thumb/4/48/075i.png/60px-075i.png" name="attachImgName" style="color: #3e81c6; text-decoration: none;" type="height=60&amp;width=60"><img border="0" height="60" src="http://images2.wikia.nocookie.net/__cb20130131074007/tos/zh/images/thumb/4/48/075i.png/60px-075i.png" style="border: 0px; margin: 5px 0px; max-width: 645px;" width="60" /></a></div>
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
本人不喜歡計數, 例如先簡單的 用<b>小魔女(B)</b>&nbsp;吃其他共5隻 地精/狼人/蜥蝪人/精靈</div>
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
把這小魔女(B)升至9-1X級</div>
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="font-family: Arial;">收集5隻等級9-1X級 的<b>小魔女</b>(B) , 每隻經驗約20000, 就拿去給要強化的主角色(A)</span></div>
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="font-family: Arial;"><br /></span></div>
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="font-family: Arial;">如果(B)材料沒有小魔女, 就拿<b>精靈系列</b>&nbsp;取代再吃其他,</span></div>
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="font-family: Arial;">只是強化經驗值會小很多 (10級左右2階精靈約10000多)</span></div>
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="font-family: Arial;"><br /></span></div>
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="font-family: Arial;">本人心態是當作主角色(A)吃6000的靈魂石吧了, 要更高的效率請參考其他文章</span></div>
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="font-family: Arial;">雖然這不是最佳效率, 但筆者本人的金錢未試過不足.</span></div>
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="font-family: Arial;"><br /></span></div>
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="font-family: Arial;"><span style="font-size: large;"><b><span style="background-color: #ffff99;">詳細解說:</span></b></span></span></div>
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="background-color: #ffff99;"><span style="font-family: Arial;">假如想升<b>主角色(A)</b>:&nbsp;</span><a class="T3_red" href="http://images2.wikia.nocookie.net/__cb20130131075423/tos/zh/images/thumb/6/60/209i.png/60px-209i.png" id="http://images2.wikia.nocookie.net/__cb20130131075423/tos/zh/images/thumb/6/60/209i.png/60px-209i.png" name="attachImgName" style="color: #3e81c6; text-decoration: none;" type="height=60&amp;width=60"><img border="0" height="60" src="http://images2.wikia.nocookie.net/__cb20130131075423/tos/zh/images/thumb/6/60/209i.png/60px-209i.png" style="border: 0px; margin: 5px 0px; max-width: 645px;" width="60" /></a><span style="font-size: small;">5星神洛奇(他沒笑就是5星!).</span></span><br />
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="background-color: #ffff99;"><span style="color: red;">錯誤範例:&nbsp;</span>直接隨便拿五隻普通的肥料(B)</span></div>
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="background-color: #ffff99;">如<a class="T3_red" href="http://images1.wikia.nocookie.net/__cb20130131074004/tos/zh/images/thumb/4/41/065i.png/60px-065i.png" id="http://images1.wikia.nocookie.net/__cb20130131074004/tos/zh/images/thumb/4/41/065i.png/60px-065i.png" name="attachImgName" style="color: #3e81c6; text-decoration: none;" type="height=60&amp;width=60"><img border="0" height="60" src="http://images1.wikia.nocookie.net/__cb20130131074004/tos/zh/images/thumb/4/41/065i.png/60px-065i.png" style="border: 0px; margin: 5px 0px; max-width: 645px;" width="60" /></a><span style="font-size: small;"><a class="T3_red" href="http://images2.wikia.nocookie.net/__cb20130131074007/tos/zh/images/thumb/4/48/075i.png/60px-075i.png" id="http://images2.wikia.nocookie.net/__cb20130131074007/tos/zh/images/thumb/4/48/075i.png/60px-075i.png" name="attachImgName" style="color: #3e81c6; text-decoration: none;" type="height=60&amp;width=60"><img border="0" height="60" src="http://images2.wikia.nocookie.net/__cb20130131074007/tos/zh/images/thumb/4/48/075i.png/60px-075i.png" style="border: 0px; margin: 5px 0px; max-width: 645px;" width="60" /></a><span style="font-size: small;"><a class="T3_red" href="http://images1.wikia.nocookie.net/__cb20130131074160/tos/zh/images/thumb/f/f5/085i.png/60px-085i.png" id="http://images1.wikia.nocookie.net/__cb20130131074160/tos/zh/images/thumb/f/f5/085i.png/60px-085i.png" name="attachImgName" style="color: #3e81c6; text-decoration: none;" type="height=60&amp;width=60"><img border="0" height="60" src="http://images1.wikia.nocookie.net/__cb20130131074160/tos/zh/images/thumb/f/f5/085i.png/60px-085i.png" style="border: 0px; margin: 5px 0px; max-width: 645px;" width="60" /></a><span style="font-size: small;"><a class="T3_red" href="http://images4.wikia.nocookie.net/__cb20130131074203/tos/zh/images/thumb/2/24/095i.png/60px-095i.png" id="http://images4.wikia.nocookie.net/__cb20130131074203/tos/zh/images/thumb/2/24/095i.png/60px-095i.png" name="attachImgName" style="color: #3e81c6; text-decoration: none;" type="height=60&amp;width=60"><img border="0" height="60" src="http://images4.wikia.nocookie.net/__cb20130131074203/tos/zh/images/thumb/2/24/095i.png/60px-095i.png" style="border: 0px; margin: 5px 0px; max-width: 645px;" width="60" /></a><span style="font-size: small;"><a class="T3_red" href="http://images2.wikia.nocookie.net/__cb20130131074536/tos/zh/images/thumb/7/74/115i.png/60px-115i.png" id="http://images2.wikia.nocookie.net/__cb20130131074536/tos/zh/images/thumb/7/74/115i.png/60px-115i.png" name="attachImgName" style="color: #3e81c6; text-decoration: none;" type="height=60&amp;width=60"><img border="0" height="60" src="http://images2.wikia.nocookie.net/__cb20130131074536/tos/zh/images/thumb/7/74/115i.png/60px-115i.png" style="border: 0px; margin: 5px 0px; max-width: 645px;" width="60" /></a><span style="font-size: small;"><span style="font-size: small;">給主角色(A)吃,</span></span></span></span></span></span></span><br />
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="font-size: small;"><span style="background-color: #ffff99;">這樣主角色(A)大約只會升幾級(1級為例可能5級也不到),花費為500元(1級時強化每隻100元)</span></span></div>
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="font-size: small;"><span style="background-color: #ffff99;"><br /></span></span></div>
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="font-size: small;"><span style="background-color: #ffff99;"><br /></span></span></div>
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="font-size: small;"><span style="background-color: #ffff99;"><span style="color: red;">建議範例:&nbsp;</span>先把肥料(B)養大一點才給主角色(A)吃</span></span></div>
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="background-color: #ffff99;">次序大約是, 先把肥料(B)如小魔女<a class="T3_red" href="http://images4.wikia.nocookie.net/__cb20130131074203/tos/zh/images/thumb/2/24/095i.png/60px-095i.png" id="http://images4.wikia.nocookie.net/__cb20130131074203/tos/zh/images/thumb/2/24/095i.png/60px-095i.png" name="attachImgName" style="color: #3e81c6; text-decoration: none;" type="height=60&amp;width=60"><img border="0" height="60" src="http://images4.wikia.nocookie.net/__cb20130131074203/tos/zh/images/thumb/2/24/095i.png/60px-095i.png" style="border: 0px; margin: 5px 0px; max-width: 645px;" width="60" /></a><span style="font-size: small;">,</span></span><br />
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="background-color: #ffff99;">先吃5隻其他經驗值比較低的肥料(C)如<a class="T3_red" href="http://images1.wikia.nocookie.net/__cb20130131074160/tos/zh/images/thumb/f/f5/085i.png/60px-085i.png" id="http://images1.wikia.nocookie.net/__cb20130131074160/tos/zh/images/thumb/f/f5/085i.png/60px-085i.png" name="attachImgName" style="color: #3e81c6; text-decoration: none;" type="height=60&amp;width=60"><img border="0" height="60" src="http://images1.wikia.nocookie.net/__cb20130131074160/tos/zh/images/thumb/f/f5/085i.png/60px-085i.png" style="border: 0px; margin: 5px 0px; max-width: 645px;" width="60" /></a><span style="font-size: small;"><a class="T3_red" href="http://images2.wikia.nocookie.net/__cb20130131074536/tos/zh/images/thumb/7/74/115i.png/60px-115i.png" id="http://images2.wikia.nocookie.net/__cb20130131074536/tos/zh/images/thumb/7/74/115i.png/60px-115i.png" name="attachImgName" style="color: #3e81c6; text-decoration: none;" type="height=60&amp;width=60"><img border="0" height="60" src="http://images2.wikia.nocookie.net/__cb20130131074536/tos/zh/images/thumb/7/74/115i.png/60px-115i.png" style="border: 0px; margin: 5px 0px; max-width: 645px;" width="60" /></a><span style="font-size: small;"><a class="T3_red" href="http://images1.wikia.nocookie.net/__cb20130131074004/tos/zh/images/thumb/4/41/065i.png/60px-065i.png" id="http://images1.wikia.nocookie.net/__cb20130131074004/tos/zh/images/thumb/4/41/065i.png/60px-065i.png" name="attachImgName" style="color: #3e81c6; text-decoration: none;" type="height=60&amp;width=60"><img border="0" height="60" src="http://images1.wikia.nocookie.net/__cb20130131074004/tos/zh/images/thumb/4/41/065i.png/60px-065i.png" style="border: 0px; margin: 5px 0px; max-width: 645px;" width="60" /></a></span></span></span><br />
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="background-color: #ffff99;">把這小魔女(B)升至7-1X級左右, 就這樣的小魔女(B)<span style="color: red;">儲5隻</span>.</span></div>
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="background-color: #ffff99;">再把<span style="color: red;">這5隻</span>7-1X級左右的小魔女(B)<a class="T3_red" href="http://images4.wikia.nocookie.net/__cb20130131074203/tos/zh/images/thumb/2/24/095i.png/60px-095i.png" id="http://images4.wikia.nocookie.net/__cb20130131074203/tos/zh/images/thumb/2/24/095i.png/60px-095i.png" name="attachImgName" style="color: #3e81c6; text-decoration: none;" type="height=60&amp;width=60"><img border="0" height="60" src="http://images4.wikia.nocookie.net/__cb20130131074203/tos/zh/images/thumb/2/24/095i.png/60px-095i.png" style="border: 0px; margin: 5px 0px; max-width: 645px;" width="60" /></a><span style="font-size: small;">給主角色(A)<a class="T3_red" href="http://images2.wikia.nocookie.net/__cb20130131075423/tos/zh/images/thumb/6/60/209i.png/60px-209i.png" id="http://images2.wikia.nocookie.net/__cb20130131075423/tos/zh/images/thumb/6/60/209i.png/60px-209i.png" name="attachImgName" style="color: #3e81c6; text-decoration: none;" type="height=60&amp;width=60"><img border="0" height="60" src="http://images2.wikia.nocookie.net/__cb20130131075423/tos/zh/images/thumb/6/60/209i.png/60px-209i.png" style="border: 0px; margin: 5px 0px; max-width: 645px;" width="60" /></a><span style="font-size: small;">吃.</span></span></span><br />
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="background-color: #ffff99;">一次大約可以把主角色(A)由1級升至15-20級,</span></div>
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="background-color: #ffff99;"><br /></span></div>
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="background-color: #ffff99;">如果用<span style="color: red;">錯誤範例</span>, 每次5小隻的肥料(B)直接給主角色(A)強化, 要把主角色(A)升至16級左右約需:</span></div>
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="background-color: #ffff99;">500元由1級升至5級, 2500元由5級升至9級左右, 4500元由9級升至13級左右</span></div>
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="background-color: #ffff99;">6500元由13級升至16級左右, 總共需要: $<span style="color: red;">14000</span>(實際應該更多)</span></div>
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="background-color: #ffff99;">如果用<span style="color: red;">建議範例</span>, 把5隻肥大了的肥料(B)給主角色(A)吃,</span></div>
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="background-color: #ffff99;">假設每隻肥大的小魔女(B)花費1500元吃其他5隻小肥料(C), $1500x5 = $7500成本</span></div>
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="background-color: #ffff99;">主角色(A)再吃這5隻小魔女(B)= $500(1級吃5隻的費用)+$7500=&nbsp;<span style="color: red;">$8000</span></span></div>
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="color: red;"><span style="background-color: #ffff99;">$14000-$8000 = 節省近4X%金錢!(這比率就算不準, 也不遠矣)</span></span></div>
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="background-color: #ffff99;">詳細的最佳效益, 請看版上的詳細分析</span></div>
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<br /></div>
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="font-family: Arial;"><b>3:</b>&nbsp;強化有機率出現[<b>超絕強化</b>] 和 [<b>完美強化</b>], 強化的經驗會多1.5倍和2倍,</span></div>
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="font-family: Arial;">強化時動畫會有點分別, 暫時不可控制這些[XX強化]的出現機率, 知道就好</span></div>
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="font-family: Arial;">超絕強化約10次出現1-2次, 完美則是1X次出現一次左右吧(個人感覺)</span></div>
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="font-family: Arial;"><br /></span></div>
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="font-family: Arial;"><b>4:&nbsp;</b>強化其次是用來把主動技能升級, 只要<b>肥料</b>跟<b>主角色</b>的主動技能是一樣(包括技能名稱),</span></div>
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="font-family: Arial;">就<span style="color: red;"><b>有機率</b>把主角色的技能等級提升</span>, 詳細請參考其他大大測試.</span></div>
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="font-family: Arial;"><br /></span></div>
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="font-family: Arial;"><b><span style="font-size: large;"><span style="color: blue;">Q: 魔法石抽卡,幾時抽最好?</span></span></b></span></div>
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="font-family: Arial;"><br /></span></div>
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="font-family: Arial;"><span style="color: red;"><span style="font-size: large;"><b>A:</b></span></span>&nbsp;有時間的話, 在這建議你無論如何也不要急</span></div>
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="font-family: Arial;"><b>例如想要暗屬性角色, 最好還是在有暗屬性卡片機率提升時抽最好</b></span></div>
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="font-family: Arial;">特別是新手, 存起1x-2x顆, 一次過抽3-5張 [感覺]會好一點</span></div>
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="font-family: Arial;">如果盲目地抽幾支, 又不同屬不同種類的可能會令你更苦惱</span></div>
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="font-family: Arial;"><br /></span></div>
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="font-family: Arial;">至於版上的女友流抽卡, 拉屎流抽卡,整點抽卡 等等純屬主觀問題在這不予置評 (?) lol</span></div>
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="font-family: Arial;">一切在於機率、人品及運氣, 本人試過連抽2張神, 也試過10抽都重複</span></div>
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="font-family: Arial;">平常心就好了</span></div>
</div>
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="font-family: Arial;"><br /></span></div>
<div style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<b><span style="font-size: large;"><span style="color: blue;">Q: 此遊戲有什麼高火力的隊伍配置?</span></span></b></div>
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<br /></div>
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="color: red;"><span style="font-size: large;"><b>A:</b></span></span></div>
</div>
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="font-family: Arial;">熱門的有 (圖片沒特別意思,舉例而已)</span></div>
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="font-family: Arial;"><a href="http://ref.gamer.com.tw/redir.php?url=http%3A%2F%2Fimages3.wikia.nocookie.net%2F__cb20130221082604%2Ftos%2Fzh%2Fimages%2Fthumb%2F9%2F93%2F208i.png%2F60px-208i.png" style="color: #3e81c6; text-decoration: none;" target="_blank"><span style="color: #3e81c6;"></span></a><span style="color: #3e81c6;"><a class="T3_red" href="http://images3.wikia.nocookie.net/__cb20130221082604/tos/zh/images/thumb/9/93/208i.png/60px-208i.png" id="http://images3.wikia.nocookie.net/__cb20130221082604/tos/zh/images/thumb/9/93/208i.png/60px-208i.png" name="attachImgName" style="color: #3e81c6; text-decoration: none;" type="height=60&amp;width=60"><img border="0" height="60" src="http://images3.wikia.nocookie.net/__cb20130221082604/tos/zh/images/thumb/9/93/208i.png/60px-208i.png" style="border: 0px; margin: 5px 0px; max-width: 645px;" width="60" /></a></span><b>純色隊</b>(由隊長 [X屬攻擊力X倍] + 幾名<b>同屬性</b>的隊員組成)</span></div>
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">

<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="font-family: Arial;">各有特色, 看隊員組合再有更多不同的變化</span></div>
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="font-family: Arial;">但暫時這遊戲可以用高火力最小可平安過渡3封, 4封用最終進化火力隊也可以平安過</span></div>
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="font-family: Arial;">5封需要接近最高進化+強化才可</span></div>

<span style="font-family: Arial;"><br /></span></div>

text-decoration: none;" type="height=60&amp;width=60"><img border="0" height="60" src="http://images1.wikia.nocookie.net/__cb20130326060334/tos/zh/images/thumb/4/45/312i.png/60px-312i.png" style="border: 0px; margin: 5px 0px; max-width: 645px;" width="60" /></a></span><a href="http://ref.gamer.com.tw/redir.php?url=http%3A%2F%2Fimages3.wikia.nocookie.net%2F__cb20130326060343%2Ftos%2Fzh%2Fimages%2Fthumb%2F4%2F41%2F314i.png%2F60px-314i.png" style="color: #3e81c6; text-decoration: none;" target="_blank"><span style="color: #3e81c6;"></span></a><span style="color: #3e81c6;"><a class="T3_red" href="http://images3.wikia.nocookie.net/__cb20130326060343/tos/zh/images/thumb/4/41/314i.png/60px-314i.png" id="http://images3.wikia.nocookie.net/__cb20130326060343/tos/zh/images/thumb/4/41/314i.png/60px-314i.png" name="attachImgName" style="color: #3e81c6; text-decoration: none;" type="height=60&amp;width=60"><img border="0" height="60" src="http://images3.wikia.nocookie.net/__cb20130326060343/tos/zh/images/thumb/4/41/314i.png/60px-314i.png" style="border: 0px; margin: 5px 0px; max-width: 645px;" width="60" /></a></span>) 的角色</span></div>
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="font-family: Arial;"><br /></span></div>
<div style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<b><span style="font-size: large;"><span style="color: blue;">Q: 我卡關了,幫忙看看我的卡有什麼問題?</span></span></b></div>
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<br /></div>
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="color: red;"><span style="font-size: large;"><b>A:</b></span></span></div>
</div>
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="font-family: Arial;">首先, 先分清楚是什麼程度的[卡關]</span></div>
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="font-family: Arial;">例如</span></div>
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="font-family: Arial;"><b>A</b>: 打怪時怪的血噴很小, 被他打一下就被秒了</span></div>
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="font-family: Arial;"><b>B</b>: 打他不痛, 他打我很痛,每次挑戰也差一點點</span></div>
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="font-family: Arial;"><br /></span></div>
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="font-family: Arial;"><b>A 的情況:</b></span></div>
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="font-family: Arial;">1:角色等級低,完全未進化 最大血量不夠</span></div>
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="font-family: Arial;">或 就算完全進化了的史來姆等等的低級卡, 帶太多也會拉低了血量(大眾臉系列)</span></div>
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="font-family: Arial;">建議先入手一些平均4-5星 等等的高級卡 (請根據上表左右的進度)</span></div>
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="font-family: Arial;"><br /></span></div>
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="font-family: Arial;">2:某些怪物本身攻擊力超高, 或是根本不預算你要吃他一發攻擊 (如4階魂)</span></div>
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="font-family: Arial;">這些往往攻擊力超高, 但攻擊回合很長, 血也比較少, 盡快打死就好</span></div>
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="font-family: Arial;">或是要用上方的保命方式去打死他</span></div>
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="font-family: Arial;"><br /></span></div>
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="font-family: Arial;"><b>B 的情況:</b></span></div>
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="font-family: Arial;">1: 可能是打王, 王一般血量比較高, 不預算你可以數回合打死,</span></div>
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="font-family: Arial;">你需要準備好來一場拉鋸戰, 例如血量可以承受他最小兩次攻擊</span></div>
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="font-family: Arial;">帶高回復力的隊員等等</span></div>
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="font-family: Arial;"><br /></span></div>
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="font-family: Arial;">2: 如你準備充足, 已盡量強化也有問題, 那可能是隊員的配法/技能不對了</span></div>
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="font-family: Arial;">例如一些高防角色(你怎打他都是扣1), 可能你要用無視防禦的技打(如<a href="http://ref.gamer.com.tw/redir.php?url=http%3A%2F%2Fimages2.wikia.nocookie.net%2F__cb20130131074655%2Ftos%2Fzh%2Fimages%2Fthumb%2F5%2F5b%2F130i.png%2F60px-130i.png" style="color: #3e81c6; text-decoration: none;" target="_blank"><span style="color: #3e81c6;"></span></a><span style="color: #3e81c6;"><a class="T3_red" href="http://images2.wikia.nocookie.net/__cb20130131074655/tos/zh/images/thumb/5/5b/130i.png/60px-130i.png" id="http://images2.wikia.nocookie.net/__cb20130131074655/tos/zh/images/thumb/5/5b/130i.png/60px-130i.png" name="attachImgName" style="color: #3e81c6; text-decoration: none;" type="height=60&amp;width=60"><img border="0" height="60" src="http://images2.wikia.nocookie.net/__cb20130131074655/tos/zh/images/thumb/5/5b/130i.png/60px-130i.png" style="border: 0px; margin: 5px 0px; max-width: 645px;" width="60" /></a></span><a href="http://ref.gamer.com.tw/redir.php?url=http%3A%2F%2Fimages4.wikia.nocookie.net%2F__cb20130221082558%2Ftos%2Fzh%2Fimages%2Fthumb%2Fe%2Fe0%2F136i.png%2F60px-136i.png" style="color: #3e81c6; text-decoration: none;" target="_blank"><span style="color: #3e81c6;"></span></a><span style="color: #3e81c6;"><a class="T3_red" href="http://images4.wikia.nocookie.net/__cb20130221082558/tos/zh/images/thumb/e/e0/136i.png/60px-136i.png" id="http://images4.wikia.nocookie.net/__cb20130221082558/tos/zh/images/thumb/e/e0/136i.png/60px-136i.png" name="attachImgName" style="color: #3e81c6; text-decoration: none;" type="height=60&amp;width=60"><img border="0" height="60" src="http://images4.wikia.nocookie.net/__cb20130221082558/tos/zh/images/thumb/e/e0/136i.png/60px-136i.png" style="border: 0px; margin: 5px 0px; max-width: 645px;" width="60" /></a></span>等)</span></div>
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="font-family: Arial;">一些低回合高攻王, 可能要配減傷/定身來減低風險</span></div>
<div align="left" style="margin: 0px; padding: 0px;">
<span style="font-family: Arial;">如沒有這些角色, 那要花時間打了</span></div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
